Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..20)
find y: int(0..5)
find z: int(0..6)

such that

!((x / (y / z)) = 10)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#order_1: bool
  find x#order_10: bool
  find x#order_11: bool
  find x#order_12: bool
  find x#order_13: bool
  find x#order_14: bool
  find x#order_15: bool
  find x#order_16: bool
  find x#order_2: bool
  find x#order_3: bool
  find x#order_4: bool
  find x#order_5: bool
  find x#order_6: bool
  find x#order_7: bool
  find x#order_8: bool
  find x#order_9: bool
new constraints:
  x#order_1

  or([!(x#order_3),x#order_2;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_4),x#order_3;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_5),x#order_4;int(1..)])

  or([!(x#order_8),x#order_7;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_9),x#order_8;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_10),x#order_9;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_11),x#order_10;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_12),x#order_11;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_13),x#order_12;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_14),x#order_13;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_15),x#order_14;int(1..)])
  or([!(x#order_16),x#order_15;int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Order, [x#order_1,x#order_2,x#order_3,x#order_4,x#order_5,x#order_6,x#order_7,x#order_8,x#order_9,x#order_10,x#order_11,x#order_12,x#order_13,x#order_14,x#order_15,x#order_16;int(1..)] [5, 20]) >= 5,SATInt(Order, [x#order_1,x#order_2,x#order_3,x#order_4,x#order_5,x#order_6,x#order_7,x#order_8,x#order_9,x#order_10,x#order_11,x#order_12,x#order_13,x#order_14,x#order_15,x#order_16;int(1..)] [5, 20]) <= 20;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
